- **sxl-api-docs**: The sixloom API and documentation loom. It writes the OpenAPI contract (contracts/openapi.yaml) from the spec, data model and system design, reconciles it with the preview's mocks, and scaffolds a GitBook-style docs site (Fumadocs, Docusaurus or Starlight) with the API reference, architecture pages, install guide and codebase tour that a newcomer reads first. Use after the system design is approved, when the user says "API docs", "/sxl-api-docs", "documentation site", or asks for an OpenAPI spec. ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/core/skills/sxl-api-docs))
- **sxl-build**: The sixloom autonomous build loom. With the preview and the API docs as the goal and the locked test suites as the stop condition, it implements the product in vertical slices with TDD, runs parallel subagents in separate git worktrees, opens a PR per slice, waits for CI to go green, merges, and repeats until every locked test passes against the real backend. It then runs sxl-validate. Use when the tests loom is approved, or when the user says "/sxl-build", "build it", "implement everything", or "run the autonomous loop". ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/core/skills/sxl-build))
- **sxl-data-model**: The sixloom data model loom (data-first design). It derives entities, attributes, relationships, constraints and lifecycle states from spec/SPEC.md and the approved preview's data needs, and writes a reviewable data-model.mdx page with a rendered ER diagram, one table per entity, and a traceability table. Use after the preview is approved, when the user says "data model", "/sxl-data-model", "design the database", or asks for entities, an ERD or a schema. ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/core/skills/sxl-data-model))
- **sxl-design**: The sixloom UI design loom. It turns spec/SPEC.md into a design system (design/DESIGN.md), a complete screen × state inventory with user flows (design/SCREENS.md), and visual comps of the key screens made with Claude Design, Google Stitch, or plain HTML. Use after the spec is approved, when the user says "design the UI", "/sxl-design", or asks for screens, flows or a design system. ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/core/skills/sxl-design))

- **sxl-loom**: The sixloom loom protocol, shared by every sxl-* skill. It says how to start a loom, iterate with the human, pass the gate, save and commit, and detect stale looms. - **sxl-patch**: Turn feedback, a bug report, a spec–preview mismatch, or a wrong locked test into a sixloom change record on the changelog stack (spec/changes/CH-xxxx-*.md). It then updates the spec, the affected artifacts and the locked tests, in that order, before any code changes. Use when the user gives feedback on the preview or the working product, reports a bug, asks to change a feature, when another loom finds a mismatch, or when the user says "/sxl-patch". ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/core/skills/sxl-patch))
- **sxl-preview**: The sixloom preview loom. It builds every screen and state in design/SCREENS.md in each of the project's interfaces (web, TUI, CLI, mobile or desktop), in the real framework, running on mock data with realistic latency and errors, so humans can review the product before any backend exists. The preview becomes the production interface. Use after the design loom, when the user says "build the preview", "front end", "TUI", "/sxl-preview", or wants a clickable prototype or mock UI. ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/core/skills/sxl-preview))
- **sxl-specify**: Write or update spec/SPEC.md, the human-confirmed list of features with stable IDs and acceptance criteria. This is the first sixloom loom. Use after `sixloom init`, when the user says "specify", "write the spec", "/sxl-specify", or brings user stories or use cases for a product to build. With --from-code, reverse-engineer a first spec from an existing codebase. ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/core/skills/sxl-specify))
- **sxl-system-design**: The sixloom high-level system design loom. It lists the services and components, their responsibilities, the data stores and integrations, and how a request flows through them, with a Mermaid diagram as the diffable source and a draw.io file for humans. It records the stack decisions as ADRs. Use after the data model is approved, when the user says "system design", "architecture", "/sxl-system-design", or asks for a services diagram. ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/core/skills/sxl-system-design))
- **sxl-tdd**: Test-driven development for sixloom builds. It runs a red → green loop in vertical slices at seams already fixed by the API contract and the locked acceptance tests, and it follows the locked-test rules. Use when implementing a slice in sxl-build, fixing a bug from sxl-patch, or whenever the user wants test-first work, red-green-refactor, or integration tests. ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/engineering/skills/sxl-tdd))
- **sxl-test-suites**: The sixloom test-suite loom, the first step of the build. It generates the acceptance suites before implementation (one e2e test per acceptance criterion and interface, in the interface's own test tool, proven green against the preview, plus contract and integration tests per API operation, red until implemented), the backend skeleton and unit-test harness, the justfile, and fast, cached, parallel GitHub Actions CI. It then locks the acceptance tests. Use when the preview and API looms are approved, or when the user says "/sxl-test-suites", "generate tests", or "set up CI". ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/core/skills/sxl-test-suites))
- **sxl-validate**: Final sixloom validation. It checks the working product against the goal (the preview and the API docs), using the full locked suite in real mode, a preview-versus-real visual comparison of every screen state, trace coverage, and an exploratory pass for gaps, then reports the findings as change records or bug slices. Use at the end of sxl-build, before a release, or when the user says "/sxl-validate", "are we done", or "find gaps". ([datamonsterr/sixloom](https://github.com/datamonsterr/sixloom/tree/HEAD/packs/core/skills/sxl-validate))

- **sy-init**: 深度阅读工作流的入口 skill。先确认知识库根目录（问题就解决了：没有指定路径时默认当前 Agent 打开的项目路径，读书笔记等一切输出文件都在该路径下组织知识库；持久化到 ~/.shendu-yuedu/config.json）与常用 markdown 编辑器（Obsidian/Typora 等，决定知识库链接规范与 log.md 初始化），再通过对话收集用户的身份背景、阅读目的、想读领域、阅读方式（纸质/电子书/微信读书等）、已有知识储备，写成《用户阅读报告.md》（OKF frontmatter）作为后续拆解/预读/精读/实践转化的共享上下文。当用户说"阅读初始化""开始读一本书""建阅读档案""我想系统读书""帮我规划阅读"时使用。也适用于用户想认真读透一本书但还没明确目标和背景的场景。这是整个阅读合集的第一步，强烈建议每个新读者从这里开始。 已安装 Huanyu-Skills-Pro（付费完整版）时，同类请求交给 Pro 版处理，本 skill 让位。 ([Huanyu-Hibiki/Huanyu-Skills](https://github.com/Huanyu-Hibiki/Huanyu-Skills/tree/HEAD/shendu-yuedu/01-init) · ★3)

- **symbiote-dependency-catalog**: Symbiote dependency-version management — read BEFORE adding a dependency, bumping a version, scaffolding a new package, or touching ANY package.json / pnpm-workspace.yaml.
